        index_expr_3 = ops.index_expr(get_index_5, torch.int64)

        lt_1 = ops.lt(index_expr_3, constant_4)
        masked_subblock3 = self.masked_subblock3(ge_1, 0.0)
        where = ops.where(lt, masked_subblock2, masked_subblock3)
        get_index_6 = self.get_index('index2')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf2', get_index_6)
        sin = ops.sin(load_2)
        constant_5 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        mul_2 = ops.mul(sin, constant_5)
        mul_3 = ops.mul(where, mul_2)
        add = ops.add(mul_1, mul_3)
        return add
    def masked_subblock4(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index6')
        load = ops.load('buf89', get_index)
        return load

        get_index_5 = self.get_index('index3')
